# 02 — Decisions and open questions

Rulings with reasons, newest first, within a day as well as between days. An overturned ruling is struck where it sits (`~~...~~`) with a note naming the entry that overturned it, and the new ruling is its own entry. A build gets an entry here only if it forced a ruling, and then the ruling alone; a build's record is its pull request, the spec's status block, and `docs/history/02-build-log.md` (the build, merge, fix, and measurement entries this file carried until the sweep of 2026-10-16, moved there whole under their own dates and headings). The index below lists every standing ruling by topic; the open questions at the end are only the open ones. The tiebreaker is `00-vision.md`. **The parents' logs do not bind here.** A parent's ruling becomes a rule of this game only when it is written in this file.

## The standing rulings, by topic

One line per ruling that stands, with the date and the entry it is from; the entry has the reason. A struck ruling is not here. Written by the sweep of 2026-10-16; a new ruling adds its line.

### The world and the map

- Upper floors are a spec (`49`): stairs are built by players and placed on the map, three tiles long, climbing one floor, the layer changing as you cross their top, and cannot be destroyed for now; third floors and root cellars under existing houses are designed in, a second floor alone is enough; nothing waits on roofs; round rooms next, roofs a distant third (2026-10-26, Upper floors: a spec)
- The demo pass: the dev chest holds every weapon and tool, the basic materials, the potions, seeds, the bearskin rug and one wide painting; your cottage 11 by 7 inside; mana regenerates 0.667 a second and its bar sits between health and stamina (2026-10-26, The demo pass)
- Any key or click wakes a sleeper early, the screen fading back in; the menu's Burn corpse works with a lit torch in hand as well as a tinderbox (2026-10-26, Waking early, and Burn corpse by a lit torch)
- Fill waterskins at a tainted source skips any skin holding clean water; an empty or tainted one still fills (2026-10-26, Fill waterskins skips clean water)
- From the playtest: a bucket boiled and poured into skins; Burn corpse for a tinderbox alone, or Use a lit torch on a body; sleep fades to black and runs slower; container items open where they lie; a stack dropped on a matching stack fills it (2026-10-26, The playtest's rulings)
- Honey goes into a clay jar made like a vial; eating it leaves the jar; a couple of jars at the cottage to start (2026-10-26, Honey goes into a clay jar)
- The mana herb is Mageroot for now; grave moss keeps its name; no plant is gathered or grows back only at night, Herbalism 6 and 7 struck and their replacements open (2026-10-25, The evening's rulings)
- Pemmican heals 3 and fills 35; eggs do not stack, open to change after a playtest (2026-10-25, The evening's rulings)
- A new rock, ore rock, or salt rock rises with a shudder, a cinematic entrance with no lore; a newborn animal may appear in view in its pen (2026-10-25, The evening's defaults ruled)
- Stand as built: the resources' counts, mushrooms as forage, an apple tree's sapling an apple tree; every spell passing tame stock by, yields by expected value, milk as a jug, the smoking rack, tanning vat, and spinning wheel not carried; the market's workbench too full for a counter; "a pair of millstones" (2026-10-25, The evening's defaults ruled)
- Ore, stone, clay, herbs, and mushrooms come back at random spots within their areas, keeping a steady count; rocks and ore rise from the ground, herbs and mushrooms appear; no herb patches; trees from saplings, sticks under trees, fish never run out (2026-10-25, Natural resources ruled)
- Animals: seven kinds; game mostly in the woods but free to roam, back slowly to a cap; livestock from the farms, led home, penned in a fenced yard; safe from the dead; fed animals produce; generous feathers; raw meat in recipes; the companion in 1.0, last (2026-10-25, Animals ruled)
- No fixed water source is clean; boiling at a lit fire by Use or either menu; the starting skin clean; the sickness a debuff, Sick (the infection's third stage renamed Queasy), 20% a tainted drink, 1% of the infection (2026-10-25, Water ruled)
- The basic lore (#217) is in the Valley's scope, paired with the map redraw (#92) and taken first (2026-10-25, The basic lore is part of the Valley's scope)
- Fixed home lights (fireplace, hearth, lantern) are switches, portable ones (torch, campfire) cost fuel; a lantern goes out; the campfire built, six logs of three hours, picked up only empty; Pick up and Take apart for everything; made by default, the village's stations one of a kind behind a world rule; Place as decoration (2026-10-25, The dead ends ruled)
- A loot table per kind of container, weights out of 32; no gear in a rolled table, placed once where it makes sense; rolled once, never refilled; boss drops personal with a rising chance (2026-10-24, The loot tables and the bags)
- A fence takes no boards, a gate included (2026-10-24, No boards on a fence)
- Everything rots, food in containers from the world's start and planted or hanging food generously slowly, within the clock law because the world's clock runs only while someone is in it; day one holds about twelve game days of food, a leaner start; a barrel doubles food's life; the starving fade over half a game day; no auto-eat; a dry growing crop pauses, a ripe one rots in the ground (2026-10-23, The food economy ruled)
- The valley's map as it stands is a testing map, not the final valley, which is redrawn roomier with distinct areas, its teachers placed with it (2026-10-23, The skills' open lines ruled)
- Each resource from the ground gets a prop of its own to mine, signposted by its look; the plan's other crops are cut from 1.0; today's broadleaf is oak, today's conifer spruce, mahogany new (2026-10-23, The skills' open lines ruled)
- A door or a window may be of another wood than its wall: `leaf`, stored only when it differs, chosen with R while building, one palette row per wall type; a region's two woods and the other's leaves in a log or stone wall are refused (2026-10-20, Doors and windows in another wood, built: the palette, the leaf, and the costs (#334, PR 2; the builder's readings, Michael's to overrule))
- The valley's buildings are in oak, spruce, mahogany, and daub, mixed, place by place in `tools/mapgen/valley.py`; Winter Pine and Bloodbark are drawn, defined, and kept out of the valley (2026-10-20, The woods as wall types, built: the valley's mix (#334, PR 1; the builder's readings, Michael's to overrule))
- Each wood (oak, spruce, mahogany, Winter Pine, Bloodbark) and daub is a wall type of its own, never paint; a wall's doors and windows match its wood by default and may be built in any other wood; oak, spruce, mahogany, and daub are the valley's and mix in its own buildings, Winter Pine and Bloodbark are restricted to their regions (2026-10-20, The woods are wall types, mixed with any wood's doors and windows; only Winter Pine and Bloodbark are restricted (Michael); costs in plain planks, the restricted woods only with Free building for now, and daub's oak doors are Primary's readings, agreed by Michael)
- A painting hangs on the camera-facing face of a plain wall, one to a segment (a wide one two segments, across a joint with no post), never at a corner, and falls to the floor as an item when its wall goes; Pick up takes it down (2026-10-20, Paintings, built: hung from the bag onto a wall's face (#326; the builder's readings, Michael's to overrule))
- Fences and gates are buildable (a plank a segment, two a gate, the hammer; Free building waives it); only the gate swings, house doors later (2026-10-20, Fences and gates are buildable now; only the gate swings, doors later (Michael); the costs, a gate only in a fence, and the swing's length are Primary's reading)
- A container does not sway, so the chicken coop is not interactable; its table, `coop`, is a placeholder, and the beehive sways with no action until there is honey (2026-10-20, The beehive and the chicken coop; the readings are the builder's, Michael's to overrule)
- One buff dish is active at a time, for now (2026-10-19, Debuffs apart from moodles; only pemmican stacks; hearty food carries buffs; the belt knife is the Dirk)
- Pemmican is the only food that stacks; every other food takes a cell each, so cooking is worth more per bag slot; hearty cooked dishes carry buffs, a food weighed by how long it keeps, how much it heals, how full it makes you, and its buff (2026-10-19, Debuffs apart from moodles; only pemmican stacks; hearty food carries buffs; the belt knife is the Dirk)
- A healing potion is not "minor" unless a major one becomes a separate item (2026-10-19, Debuffs apart from moodles; only pemmican stacks; hearty food carries buffs; the belt knife is the Dirk)
- The wizard is alive, a teacher; a skill's level gate stops a player harvesting the food it gates, ripe fields included; every container's loot table is provisional until ruled, and the loot tables are likely the Valley scope's last step (Michael: "this might be the last thing") (2026-10-19, The valley has no cure; the wizard lives; the witch teaches Potions; gates stop a harvest; every loot table provisional; two old limits struck)
- Unsafe water always quenches thirst and never guarantees a penalty: a drink may bring a small sickness that passes (more tired, slower to heal), and carries a small real chance of the infection, a second way in beside a bite; the waterskin sips by itself only clean water, and unsafe water may still be carried; crops take any water (2026-10-19, Unsafe water: a sickness that may come and passes; the skin sips only clean water; crops take any water)
- All natural water, water tiles and wells, is unsafe until purified; ~~how it is purified is open (#279; open question 34)~~ (answered 2026-10-25, Water ruled: boiling at a lit fire, `docs/44`) (2026-10-19, Natural water is unsafe until purified; mana; drain heals never replace food; a region gates its spells; what it does narrowed (2026-10-19, Unsafe water: a sickness that may come and passes; the skin sips only clean water; crops take any water))
- Michael hopes a region is not done in one sitting and then visited only for one ingredient: it is returned to (2026-10-18, Health is a thousand; drops by chance; armour modest; weapons differ by situation)
- How long the valley's day-one food would keep a player from starving is a statistic kept current on the wiki and a target in design; the starting foods are weak on purpose, cooking improves them, and the regions could offer better (2026-10-18, Day-one food is a tracked statistic; what scripting means)
- A villager the dead have hurt heals slowly at the player's rate and still talks and barks; only a dead one is silent (2026-10-18, A hurt villager heals at the player's rate; 2026-10-18, A wounded villager still talks and barks)
- An item's name is specific unless the thing is truly one of a kind; identical materials keep plain names (2026-10-18, The Onion-head Hat; real-world references on one page; specific names; a lore issue)
- An action that would realistically need a tool needs the tool carried; in a hand too is open; the bag's size is the puzzle, the Developer Bag perhaps excepted (2026-10-18, An action that needs a tool needs the tool with you)
- A villager who teaches a skill is immune to the dead, like the guide, by a flag on each villager; some villagers stay at risk (2026-10-18, The valley first; food heals a little; teachers are safe; the wizard; the dead strike what they break)
- Food heals what it says on the tin, eaten full or not; hunger stops at the top; no cap or scaling on a heal; a well-fed body heals faster on its own (2026-10-18, Food heals what it says on the tin; a full belly heals faster)
- Food heals a little, a stopgap; only potions heal big; cooked and combined food more, by default (2026-10-18, The valley first; food heals a little; teachers are safe; the wizard; the dead strike what they break)
- The dead strike what they break, an attack on a cooldown as on a player, Michael's leaning and the default; the shake toned down (2026-10-18, The valley first; food heals a little; teachers are safe; the wizard; the dead strike what they break)
- Magic is taught by a friendly wizard in the valley (2026-10-18, The valley first; food heals a little; teachers are safe; the wizard; the dead strike what they break); Potions by the witch (2026-10-19, The valley has no cure; the wizard lives; the witch teaches Potions; gates stop a harvest; every loot table provisional; two old limits struck)
- A torch burns down while lit (fastest in hand, slower on a belt, bracket, or post; a kill costs more), is put out and lit with a tinderbox, and starts unlit; belt and hand never stack; the village's lights are lanterns ~~that never go out~~ (overturned 2026-10-25, The dead ends ruled: a lantern goes out) (2026-10-17, Torches burn down; the tinderbox; the village's lights become lanterns)
- Only bodies burn; anything with fire (a torch, a tinderbox, a fireball) lights what is made to be lit; nothing spreads; the fireball keeps some damage, less than now, and becomes a flare (2026-10-17, Only characters burn; fire lights what is made to be lit; the fireball as a flare)
- A sealed room is safe, and a cell: nothing digs, no wall breaks, you cannot leave (2026-10-17, The siege: a sealed room is a cell, the dead wait for dawn, a crowd bursts a fence); narrowed 2026-10-18 (One reach test for every action, #208): it binds the dead, not the player, who may stamp a door into a wall or Remove a wall they built, from either side ("removing wall and placing door are fine")
- The dead that know you are inside wait until the next dawn, for now; no sleep while they do (2026-10-17, The siege: a sealed room is a cell, the dead wait for dawn, a crowd bursts a fence)
- A fence bursts into splinters, to one of the dead alone in about 140 s, faster to a crowd (2026-10-17, The siege: a sealed room is a cell, the dead wait for dawn, a crowd bursts a fence; overturned in part 2026-10-18 on #185: one alone can)
- A fence takes a few of the dead long enough to run or arm (real bodies: one alone about 140 s, two 71 s, three 48 s, eight 37 s), and the dead bite over a fence (2026-10-17, The siege: a sealed room is a cell, the dead wait for dawn, a crowd bursts a fence)
- Built stone walls are unbreakable but cost a great deal of stone; every entrance a player can use is one the dead can use, and whatever closes it they can break (2026-10-17, The siege: a sealed room is a cell, the dead wait for dawn, a crowd bursts a fence)
- New kinds of monster that reach the valley because players explore other regions press on doors; more pressure and more ways to defend should both come from exploring (2026-10-17, The siege: a sealed room is a cell, the dead wait for dawn, a crowd bursts a fence)
- Shutters are buildable; how doors and windows are built again is the build's (2026-10-17, The siege: a sealed room is a cell, the dead wait for dawn, a crowd bursts a fence)
- Nobody climbs through a window, player or dead; a window is never a safe spot from the dead (2026-10-16, Nobody climbs through a window; region is the word)
- "Region" is the word for a place beyond the valley (2026-10-16, Nobody climbs through a window; region is the word)
- The guide stands beside the spawn on every map, with a reason to be there, written like anyone (2026-10-16, The needs-michael queue)
- Darkness shortens sight: the dead fade to hidden past a soft edge unless in light; a torch gives it back (2026-10-16, The needs-michael queue)
- A shed, hut, or forest cabin is one room; the two-rooms rule is for houses (2026-10-16, The needs-michael queue)
- A world rule is general to any map, never a change to one map's layout in one way (2026-10-16, The menus and the site)
- Trees and Starting population are per-world rules, chosen at New world and locked after (2026-10-16, The menus and the site)
- A world rule is baked in at creation or tweakable on the fly, and the screens say which (2026-10-16, The menus and the site)
- A hand-authored world with procedural filling of the spaces it leaves empty: a direction, not a wall (2026-10-16, The four lines of 00-vision.md, ruled)
- One continuous map with real line of sight; chunks, rooms by flood fill, never authored (2026-10-08, The project exists)
- Fire and resources are the economy; nothing shuts off on a timer; farming is load-bearing (2026-10-08, The project exists)
- Villagers are killable, carry no weapons, never fight for the player; they flee, follow, and stay (2026-10-08, The project exists; teachers and the guide excepted from killable, 2026-10-18)
- Medieval, low fantasy, no guns; dark medieval fantasy, not strictly medieval (2026-10-08, The project exists; 2026-10-09, Milestone 1 passed, and the vibe pass)
- Trees block only their trunk; the dead know only where they last saw you; a bite needs line of sight (2026-10-09, Milestone 1 passed, and the vibe pass; the search narrowed by the siege, 2026-10-17, The siege: a sealed room is a cell, the dead wait for dawn, a crowd bursts a fence)
- Authored structure, scattered filler: sets with padding rings, features, scatter by rule, the wild beyond; harvest first (2026-10-09, Milestone 1 passed, and the vibe pass)
- Shutters on every window: tap E opens and closes; closed shutters block sight; doors start closed; fittings one wood (2026-10-09, Milestone 1 passed, and the vibe pass)
- Torches mount in brackets and on posts, never on the ground (2026-10-09, afternoon, Torches, fire, hands, and the clock)
- At night the dead see fire from far away; fires are data; a lit hearth lights its whole room (2026-10-09, afternoon, Torches, fire, hands, and the clock)
- Raw WebGL2 for the world, Canvas 2D for the dev art; portals are emitters with a transmission scalar (2026-10-09, night, The renderer goes to WebGL)
- Openings are boards only, read from the attacker's side; everything spent, nothing repaired; nothing breaks a wall, save a fence to the dead (one alone slowly, a crowd faster) (2026-10-10, Openings; 2026-10-17, The siege: a sealed room is a cell, the dead wait for dawn, a crowd bursts a fence)
- The dead attack the room's nearest opening, never a window as their way in, or, by default, the one they saw you use (2026-10-10, Openings; narrowed 2026-10-16, Nobody climbs through a window; region is the word; narrowed 2026-10-17, The siege: a sealed room is a cell, the dead wait for dawn, a crowd bursts a fence)
- Water is unwalkable by everything; shallow crossings are slow for all (2026-10-12, Water is unwalkable by everything)
- The 1.0 map is a 256 by 256 valley, generated from a description per place, never typed (2026-10-12, The 1.0 map's first rulings; 2026-10-12, Step 0 of `30` built)
- Floors and walls carry their rules as data; a see-through wall bounds no room (2026-10-12, Step 0 of `30` built)
- Dev art for everything; every box says what it is; nothing waits for art (2026-10-12, The big pass is approved as written)
- Reading never grows a chunk; growth is the ring of four round each player and the mutators (2026-10-13, Reading never grows a chunk)
- Far wild chunks past eight are dropped and their dead sleep with them (2026-10-12, Far wild chunks are dropped)
- The simulation rolls only through `rng.js` and reads time only from `loop.simTime()` (2026-10-12, The simulation gets one generator and one clock)
- Gatherables wobble and are outlined, trees excused from the outline; a walk through a crop sways it (2026-10-11, The ground is accepted; the gatherables law; 2026-10-12, Walking through a field makes it wobble)
- One poke: a left click swings and sways, a right click sways and opens actions; clicks reach 2.6 tiles (2026-10-11, One poke for everything you can touch; 2026-10-12, Clicks reach 2.6 tiles)
- Only trees are seen through; walls have their own fade (2026-10-11, Only trees are seen through)
- The ground is baked per chunk; cobble inside a dirt verge; authored surfaces cover their tiles (2026-10-11, The ground is scoped: milestone 9; 2026-10-11, Authored surfaces cover their tiles)
- Mushrooms, rocks, pumpkins, and crops are game objects; the well is a fixture with a use (2026-10-11, Mushrooms and rocks become game objects that wiggle; 2026-10-11, The well's bucket; milestone 10 done)
- Walls are 80 px tall, stone 16 px thick and wood 14; doors a hair shorter; openings fixed in pixels (2026-10-11, Walls 80 px tall and 18 px thick; 2026-10-11, Wall thickness per material)
- Open shutters fold back about 160 degrees; open doors stand at 90 (2026-10-11, Cottage shutters, and a wall height preview)
- A post wherever the material changes, in the heavier; boards are drawn overlays; facings a drawing and its mirror (2026-10-11, The building batch arrived; 2026-10-11, Rulings on the village art plan)
- Furniture is solid (2026-10-12, The engine upgrade is the next task)
- Carry anything not rooted, built in, or burning; a log wall costs logs; removal on X refunds nothing (2026-10-11, build, Placement: carry rule, log wall, free building; 2026-10-11, build, Removal and longer reach)
- X removes only what players made; the wheelbarrow is carried like a barrel (2026-10-15, The wiki census's surprises ruled)
- Needs are hunger, thirst, and sleep; no cold until seasons, never boredom; a dry crop pauses (2026-10-12, The big pass answered; overturned in part 2026-10-18, Food rots: a pace, not world decay: food rots)
- Food rots: a design tool for pacing and upkeep, like a torch burning down, never world decay; its rates and the ways to preserve food open (#264) (2026-10-18, Food rots: a pace, not world decay)
- Stations take ingredients from everything in reach, work while you walk off or sleep; a fire is maintenance (narrowed 2026-10-25, The dead ends ruled: a station's and a portable fire; a home's fire is a switch) (2026-10-12, The big pass answered; 2026-10-13, Step 3 of `30` built)
- "Nothing interesting happens" is the default of any item on anything (2026-10-12, The big pass answered)
- Durability is kills, and low; tools are poor weapons of five kills (2026-10-12, The big pass answered; 2026-10-13, Step 3 of `30` built)
- The bucket is an item, in the bag, the belt, or a hand, filled at water and poured on a plot (2026-10-18, The bucket goes in the bag)
- Sleep fast-forwards the clock in a bed indoors, never interrupted; drink and fill from any water; ~~two animals, last~~ (superseded 2026-10-19, The valley has no cure; the wizard lives; the witch teaches Potions; gates stop a harvest; every loot table provisional; two old limits struck: Beastlore, #289) (2026-10-12, The big pass answered; no sleep during a siege, 2026-10-17, The siege: a sealed room is a cell, the dead wait for dawn, a crowd bursts a fence)
- Survival is on by default; the rates are to the day; waking is rested and morning (2026-10-13, Step 2 of `30` built)

### Combat

- Sight-pull: one of the dead first turning to hunt a player calls once to the dead near it that see it, and those never call others; sound draws no one (2026-10-25, Sight-pull ruled)
- Throwing knives do 18 damage, not 15 (2026-10-26, Throwing knives do 18)
- Throwing knives pull and axes stop: the knife 18 (15 until 2026-10-26) and 30% to break, the axe 30, always staggering, knocking down at 0.35, 10% to break; they spin, and land or lodge like arrows (2026-10-25, Throwing knives and axes ruled)
- Potions, poisons, and mana as `47`: clay vials, clean water, two herbs and a secondary at a cauldron, mana 100, the Fire Bolt 20 mana, poisons put on a blade or arrows with Use, a splash for every potion (2026-10-25, Potions, poisons, and mana ruled)
- Repairs run out: one count per item, the grindstone to 60, 40, 20%, the anvil to 100, 80, 60%; Burn corpse destroys a body and all in it (2026-10-25, The dead ends ruled)
- The melee feel pass accepted as the baseline; tuning goes on with the skills (2026-10-24, The melee baseline)
- The shove pushes 0.65 tiles, a tired one 0.45 (`shove.tiredPush`); the tired stagger stays under the shove's recovery (2026-10-24, The shove pushes farther)
- The dead that wait for a bite token strike the fence or gate between them and their target; the shove's push 0.4 tiles; a placeholder reel pose through a stagger; the downed keep their health bar and are outlined; a closing burst near a seen target, a trial; corners stay dangerous; the spear, the halberd, the bow, and the dead's 150 left for now (2026-10-24, The first real melee test)
- The spear breaks one throw in four, the same on a hit or a miss, and the thrower is told; a spear that stays in a body keeps its wear (2026-10-24, The spear breaks one throw in four, hit or miss, and says so)
- The melee feel pass (`docs/41`, #89), six steps adopted across the board: the bite gets a 0.4 s wind-up and any landed hit in it cancels the bite; every hit shows a flinch, the stagger still by weight; the shove on Space is the crowd tool (three targets, a bigger push, a recovery in which no swing starts); knockdown is restricted to the mace, the halberd's thrust, and the shove, and a rising zombie is safe from the finisher; stamina regeneration 10 a second with swing costs raised is the one pacer; bite tokens let only a few of the dead in reach bite at once, per target; the dead's health is set from hit counts after the rest (2026-10-23, The melee feel pass)
- No endurance tiers and no muscle strain, ever; the pacing is one stamina bar (2026-10-23, The melee feel pass)
- The player's turn rate is never capped; the commitment to a swing is a slowdown through its animation and a held facing (2026-10-23, The melee feel pass)
- Melee combat testing is not finished until hit sounds are in; the flinch is the hit sound's visual twin (2026-10-23, The melee feel pass)
- Death takes all skills, the stakes that make a life a run; a sandbox rule to soften it may come later (2026-10-23, The skills' open lines ruled)
- Combat experience: Melee, Ranged, and Magic by damage dealt with that kind, Faith by its acts and a smite's own damage; a skill touches combat exactly as far as the four combat trees say (2026-10-23, The skills' open lines ruled)
- Every melee weapon is craftable, since weapons degrade and repair only delays it; the staff in either hand and the book in the other, the staff degrades and the book never, owning a book grants its spell, no spell bar; Fire Bolt is the fireball's name; Life Ward a Faith 3 book; the cure by Cure Undeath or a Divine Potion (2026-10-23, The skills' open lines ruled)
- Smite deals 150%, 200%, then 250% of the hit that triggered it as holy spell damage (Faith 2, 5, 9), so it scales with Holy Might; praying at an altar restores mana and stamina from Faith 1, then also removes bleeds, then also restores health, spaced out by level; Consecrate Ground lights its whole area (2026-10-20, Faith: Smite is a share of the hit; prayer grows by level; Consecrate Ground gives light (Michael); the levels of prayer's later steps are Primary's reading)
- The valley holds no cure for the infection: it is found beyond the valley (2026-10-19, The valley has no cure; the wizard lives; the witch teaches Potions; gates stop a harvest; every loot table provisional; two old limits struck)
- The best item a production skill makes is never the best of its kind in the game: the best weapons, armour, and bags come from bosses; repairing is a stop-gap that makes a thing last longer, never a reason to stop making new ones (2026-10-19, A production skill's best is never the best of its kind; repair is a stop-gap)
- The cure for the infection is a mid- to late-game option, gated by skill, never something made on the first day (2026-10-19, The cure for the infection is mid to late game, gated by skill)
- A boss may drop a rare, very powerful capstone item worth farming it for: its power and especially its mechanical novelty: a policy on the condition that such items can be found (2026-10-19, Rare capstone drops worth farming a boss for, a policy on its condition)
- A book in the off hand costs the shield; within the staff-and-book proposal (#275), the Holy Symbol is Faith's permission, cast with the symbol raised, and utility books compete with spell books for room (2026-10-19, The belt restated and added to; a book in the off hand costs the shield; the Holy Symbol is Faith's staff, within the proposal)
- Mana exists, for spells: slow natural regeneration, potions to top it up, a free top-up at an altar, perhaps special foods later; a spell that drains health is a utility heal on a cooldown or a high mana cost and never replaces food or potions; Blizzard and Consecrate do small damage around you, their point is utility; a region's spells are gated by the region, never by level alone, for now (2026-10-19, Natural water is unsafe until purified; mana; drain heals never replace food; a region gates its spells)
- A player's health stays a hundred, the ×10 scale withdrawn for now; the dead and bosses scale as far as they need, thousands for a boss; max health growing with levels and gear is Michael's leaning, not ruled (2026-10-19, A player's health stays a hundred, for now; the dead scale freely)
- Gear can drop by chance, a lever never used before; a boss's best drop is not guaranteed every kill (2026-10-18, Health is a thousand; drops by chance; armour modest; weapons differ by situation)
- The least armour is just better than nothing; armour mostly in flat numbers, a percent rarely; how strong the best gets is open (2026-10-18, Health is a thousand; drops by chance; armour modest; weapons differ by situation)
- Few strictly-better items does not mean every weapon does the same damage everywhere: resistances make a weapon strong in one place and weak in another (2026-10-18, Health is a thousand; drops by chance; armour modest; weapons differ by situation)
- ~~Combat stays paused until Michael's play session; then the spin cap, knockdown, and push per hit~~ (superseded 2026-10-23, The melee feel pass: Michael played, and ruled the pass; the spin cap is not built, knockdown is restricted, the push per hit stays as it is) (2026-10-16, The needs-michael queue)
- ~~The knockdown chance 0.3 is high and stands until the combat pass re-rules it~~ (re-ruled 2026-10-23, The melee feel pass: 0 by default, a chance only on the mace, the halberd's thrust, and the shove) (2026-10-16, The sweep, after wave 3)
- The swing resolves on the click, the animation is cosmetic, the cooldown is the rate limit (2026-10-11, Placement and melee scoped)
- Left click swings, Space shoves; a swing ~~at zero stamina~~ the bar cannot pay for lands at half damage (narrowed 2026-10-24, Primary's reading in building `41` step 4, Michael's to overrule: a swing the bar cannot pay for lands at half; with regeneration between swings the bar never reached zero, so the old test never fired) (2026-10-11, Placement and melee scoped)
- Every number on a weapon is tunable per item; knockback and the shove's push are a third (2026-10-11, Melee tunables, the shove, and multi-hit; 2026-10-11, Melee's first play notes)
- A floating health bar is the finisher's tell; swing targets are a green outline, a setting (2026-10-11, A floating health bar is the finisher's tell; 2026-10-11, Swing targets, and a pin for a sandbox screen)
- Left the main hand; right tapped the menu, held the off hand; middle or Q the other mode (2026-10-12, The mouse in combat: three roles)
- Spear and shield is special: stab, block, throw; a spear never beside another one-handed weapon; the throw may break it (2026-10-12, The mouse in combat: three roles)
- The bow brace: hold right to move slower and draw faster; a tap of right cancels a draw (2026-10-12, The mouse in combat: three roles)
- The guard: total in front, half at the sides, 35 percent from behind; 5 stamina, none regenerates while up (2026-10-12, The guard: total in front)
- Dual wielding is on the left button; an off-hand swing exposes you for 2.5 s (2026-10-12, Dual wielding on the left button)
- Stagger by weapon weight, then 0.6 s immunity; ~~a bite under way is cancelled only by an interrupting weapon~~ (narrowed 2026-10-23, The melee feel pass: a bite in its wind-up is cancelled by any landed hit, `interrupts` retired) (2026-10-12, Stagger by weight, immunity)
- The knockdown is the game's crit; the third-hit rule is off; the finisher is 1.5 (2026-10-12, The knockdown is the game's crit; 2026-10-12, The third-hit rule off for testing)
- Multi-hit stays a setting, off by default (2026-10-12, Multi-hit is the easy button)
- The mace is the strongest, the sword second; the halberd is two-handed with a thrust on the middle button (2026-10-12, The weapon order, and the halberd; 2026-10-12, The halberd's thrust and a longer picture)
- Free draw 1.4 s, braced 0.6; arrows that hit lodge and return in the loot; the guide a setting (2026-10-12, The guard shows; the bow's free draw slowed; 2026-10-12, Arrows that hit stay in the body)
- The throwing spear is the arrow thrown by hand; nothing is left in the hand (2026-10-12, The throwing spear is the arrow thrown by hand)
- Bleed stacks to five from bites, by Michael's two bounds; a bandage closes it (2026-10-12, Bleed stacks, with Michael's two bounds)
- The infection is a marker: 20 percent a landed bite, 1.5 days, four stages, no effect yet (2026-10-12, The virus comes back as a marker); a drink of unsafe water is a second way in, a small chance (2026-10-19, Unsafe water: a sickness that may come and passes; the skin sips only clean water; crops take any water)
- Combat difficulty and infection are one dial, set together (2026-10-12, Combat difficulty and infection are one dial)
- Weapon durability is one-way, by landed hits, evaporates (2026-10-12, Weapon durability: permanent, hits only, evaporates; overturned in part (2026-10-19, A production skill's best is never the best of its kind; repair is a stop-gap): repair at the anvil as a stop-gap)
- ~~Hold the spin-click turn cap~~ (closed 2026-10-23, The melee feel pass: no cap; the commitment is a slowdown through the swing), leave bleed as it is, leave the infection as the marker (2026-10-12, Combat changes paused, deliberately)
- The dead may hunt villagers, a setting; the player always first (2026-10-12, The dead may hunt villagers)
- The fireball needs a staff and the Book of Fire both held: the bow's mechanic, flat, big, and slow (2026-10-14, The fireball: a staff and a spellbook cast it; 2026-10-14, The fireball bigger and slower)
- No friendly fire; the fireball's burst counts only a body ahead of the cast (2026-10-15, No friendly fire; the fireball's burst only ahead)
- Burning: 0.8 a second for 10 s, capped at 10, only the dead; fire arrows dipped at a lit fire (2026-10-15, Burning made real)
- A weapon's `ignite` chance is on its block; the torch's is one in four (2026-10-15, Burning made real)
- A dead player lies as a body you can search (2026-10-14, A dead player lies as a body you can search)
- Zombie aggro is on by default; ~~Combat testing places a hundred dead in a ring~~ (superseded 2026-10-16: The menus and the site, #130: it retired for the Starting population rule) (2026-10-15, The wiki census's surprises ruled)

### The character, the figure, and art

- A character is right-handed or left-handed, never ambidextrous, right by default, chosen once with its identity; it is visual and a label alone: the hand slots' names on the character screen, the belt's hand icons, and the arm the main hand's thing is drawn in; everything mechanical stays main and off (2026-10-25, Right- or left-handed)
- Cheery at home, spooky beyond: the valley start-as-Stardew, Tirisfal the outer regions' register, region by region, not a gradient (2026-10-25, Cheery at home, spooky beyond)
- Habbo is no longer the visual reference; its techniques (flat side shading, stepped shadows) stay as how pixels are drawn (2026-10-25, Cheery at home, spooky beyond)
- The long, thin items (the plank, the stick, the shovel, the hatchet) take the larger bag icon, the drawing rendered to fill the cell; the rope belt is drawn in today's belt's browns (2026-10-20, The everyday items' art: the large icons for the long items; the rope belt in today's belt's browns (Michael))
- A painted head item in two tones is dyed like hair by its entry's dye, undyed or of more tones it keeps the paint; the dead draw an item's painted layer too (2026-10-18, The Onion-head Hat's art: the brow; a dyed hood is dyed like hair, and the dead wear it too)
- The weeping willow is cut; the art generators are to be archived in tools/art-gen, never run without Michael (2026-10-16, The needs-michael queue)
- Throwables and consumables usually stack, case by case; one-offs like the Book and Staff of Fire never do (2026-10-16, The needs-michael queue); narrowed (2026-10-19, Debuffs apart from moodles; only pemmican stacks; hearty food carries buffs; the belt knife is the Dirk): of the foods, only pemmican stacks
- A waterskin carried, belt or bag, sips by itself at Thirsty, the emptiest first, never mid-fight (2026-10-16, Auto-drinking); narrowed (2026-10-19, Unsafe water: a sickness that may come and passes; the skin sips only clean water; crops take any water): only clean water
- Cute wins over grim; the dead are drawn like everyone else (2026-10-08, The project exists)
- Dev art carries the identity: spooky medieval, never permanently night; occlusion is a hole, not a fade (2026-10-09, Milestone 1 passed, and the vibe pass)
- The figure is a paper doll on a small rig, generated, eight facings, none mirrored; outfits bake into one sheet (2026-10-10, The figure)
- The figures are goobers, not humans; they and them for every figure (2026-10-10, night, Goobers, and five pins)
- The body is the rig's, not touched again without a ruling; the design agent draws what goes on it (2026-10-12, The body stays the rig's; 2026-10-12, We have a body)
- The design agent paints parts on the template, one slot per file, the garment alone; the rig does the animations (2026-10-12, The slicer, and the rules the design agent paints by; 2026-10-12, The characters redone: a brief drafted)
- No undergarment; a hat ends where a hard thing begins; one body; stubble never; a dress shows the shoes (2026-10-12, No undergarment; 2026-10-12, The character brief's last four questions)
- Every item delivery carries the world art and the inventory icon, and a portrait if held or worn (2026-10-12, The first item art arrives; 2026-10-12, The bag and the character screen are two panels)
- The character screen is the identity; the dead tones allowed; bald first; facial hair after 1.0 (the wizard's beard open, 2026-10-18, The valley first; food heals a little; teachers are safe; the wizard; the dead strike what they break); no body shapes (2026-10-13, The character screen is the identity)
- The trees' tiers are remapped and drawn bigger, giants one in five; half the debris; a thinner forest (2026-10-11, The trees' tiers are remapped and drawn bigger; 2026-10-11, Half the debris; 2026-10-11, Fewer mushrooms, sticks under trees)
- The one-handed swing is a high-to-fall slash, never behind the back; a pose has a wrist (2026-10-13, The one-handed swing is a high-to-fall slash; 2026-10-13, A swing has a wrist)
- Better animations are poses as data on the final rig, not more frames; no dual-wield pose but the off hand's swing (2026-10-13, Better animations, scoped; 2026-10-25, The off hand's swing moves the off arm)
- The off hand's swing moves the off arm: the main swing's pose mirrored, not a pose set of its own (2026-10-25, The off hand's swing moves the off arm)
- Elbows on the rig, never knees, held straight until a pose uses them; no art redrawn (2026-10-15, Elbows on the rig, held straight)
- A bow's limbs are upright on screen whatever way it points; a staff is held like a vertical pole (2026-10-14, The bow held upright, both hands on it; 2026-10-15, The bowstring to the chin and the cast's pose)

### The interface and the screens

- No browser dialog: every question is asked on the page, never through window.alert, confirm, or prompt; tools/check.js scans for them (2026-10-26, No browser dialog)
- "Put out", never "douse"; a wave emote for the demo; immune villagers cower; the dye vat's picker and per-item colour schemes (2026-10-25, The dead ends ruled)
- One thing a cell: only arrows, throwing knives and axes, bandages, seeds, and pemmican stack; bags made, never found, the satchel, the rucksack, and the valley's best in tiers (2026-10-24, The loot tables and the bags)
- The aim guide on by default, off for the challenge; a mute key on F8 with a line as its twin; the player on grass steps as the dead shuffle; sounds step 2 widened to the shove, the bow, the cast, the dead's death and fall, doors and shutters (2026-10-24, The first real melee test)
- A new character knows the level-1 recipes; a level's recipes are learned once from the skill's teacher on the teacher's own screen; the station panel lists every recipe of the station, the unlearned greyed with what they need; buffs and debuffs are specific, interactable effects shown in the debuff row, never moodles, which describe a general state (2026-10-23, The skills' open lines ruled)
- A rug is an item laid from the bag into a slot of its own under whatever stands on it, picked up again unless furniture is on it; E takes it up only when nothing else is in reach (2026-10-20, Rugs, built)
- Text at or under 15 UI px is set in the system's clear sans-serif, the rest in the pixel font; a Display setting, Small text, flips it (2026-10-20, Very small text in a clear sans-serif; the readings are the builder's, Michael's to overrule)
- Paintings and rugs are items: carried in the bag, placed with a right-click Place and an outline where they will go, picked up again with a right-click, found in the world and brought home (2026-10-20, Paintings and rugs are items: placed from the bag, picked up again (Michael); the bag sizes, a rug that won't come up from under furniture, and the art's outline were Primary's readings, agreed by Michael the same day; where they come from now and who can pick one up are still Primary's readings)
- Every digit is in the clear sans at any size, letters keeping their face; Small text's Pixel restores the old look (2026-10-20, Every digit in the clear font, at any size (Michael); Pixel's digits are Primary's reading)
- Every item in a grid or slot has a hover card, WoW's tooltip: a small dark rounded box with the full name, the kind, and the direct uses read from the data, Use in gold, following the pointer (2026-10-19, The item hover card; the law is 2026-10-18)
- A Drink from unsafe water says "(tainted)", every source until purifying exists; a crop has Inspect, a line of what it is and how it stands with its tile marked, this client's own; the Harvest labels name the crop; Object Highlight, a dev setting on by default, outlines the game object under the pointer (2026-10-19, Unsafe water says so; a crop can be inspected; Object Highlight, a dev setting (Michael); the trough and the font tainted, Inspect this client's own, and the playtest's reading are Primary's, Michael's to overrule; what Object Highlight outlines and leaves out is the builder's reading, 2026-10-19, Object Highlight (#301))
- Floating numbers over heads: a red -X for a hit, a green +X for a heal, a skill's icon with the number gained for a gather in white or black by the light, drawn from events the authority records, not from health diffs; a number over a body I cannot see is hidden; the fourteen skill icons are placeholder art in `ui/skill-icons.js` (2026-10-19, Floating numbers over heads, and an icon for each skill; the readings are the builder's, Michael's to overrule)
- Moodles are for ongoing general needs (hunger, thirst, tiredness) and the infection; a specific negative effect, a bleed or a poison, is a debuff of its own, WoW-style (2026-10-19, Debuffs apart from moodles; only pemmican stacks; hearty food carries buffs; the belt knife is the Dirk); bleeding is the first, a row at the top right, its time in minutes rounded up and seconds under a minute (2026-10-19, Bleeding is a debuff: the row at the top right; the readings are the builder's, Michael's to overrule)
- The belt's slots have roles: the rope belt a main-hand, an off-hand, and the one utility slot, which never expands, the leather belt one more of each hand; a hand slot's key draws into its hand and swaps (a two-hander from a main-hand slot into both hands) or eats, the utility slot eats and stores anything in one cell; a two-hander refused in every off-hand slot; a tap on an empty hand slot does nothing; every failed slot action gives a line; a tap never drinks a skin; no hand chosen per slot (2026-10-25, The belt's slots have roles)
- The belt's key order is each player's own setting, Belt slot order, Sequential by default (1 main, 2 off, 3 utility), the key sent as the slot's role; each empty slot stamped faint with a hand or a pouch, its hover card saying what it is for (2026-10-25, The belt's slots have roles)
- The belt carries a lantern for more light than a torch's (2026-10-19, The belt restated and added to; a book in the off hand costs the shield; the Holy Symbol is Faith's staff, within the proposal); no left-click parity: a left press on a slot is a drag (2026-10-19, A belt slot's key does one thing, into the hand chosen for that slot; no left-click parity)
- Every item in a grid or slot has a hover card with at least its full name and its direct uses, read from the data by default; "Use" capitalised and in the Use mode's gold (2026-10-18, Every item in a grid has a hover card); a change to what an item does is checked against its card in the same change (2026-10-20, A change to an item checks its card (Michael))
- An eye on each worn slot hides that item's art and keeps every use; the choice stays with the slot across re-equips, by default (2026-10-18, Hiding a worn item's art: an eye on each worn slot)
- ~~The aim guide is off by default, for the bow, a throw, and the cast~~ (overturned 2026-10-24, The first real melee test: on by default, off for the challenge) (2026-10-16, The aim guide is off by default)
- Talk's panel is entirely click-based, with topics, wiki links, Continue, and That's all for now; E starts it (2026-10-16, The needs-michael queue)
- Never draw UI into the canvas; the interface has its own scale, like a web page (2026-10-10, night, The view, the screens, and a law)
- The world fills the window; nothing is laid out against 960×540 (2026-10-10, night, The view, the screens, and a law)
- If the game is good, people will sit for a loading screen (2026-10-10, night, The view, the screens, and a law)
- The zoom snaps between three levels: wide 1.1, middle 1.8, close 2.7 (2026-10-10, late, The belt as utility slots, a belt torch, the zoom)
- The belt is utility slots for consumables; a belt torch gives a little over half its light (0.6 since 2026-10-17, was half), seen from a little over half as far (2026-10-10, late, The belt as utility slots, a belt torch, the zoom; 2026-10-17, A belt torch sees a bit further than none); restated and added to (2026-10-19, The belt restated and added to; a book in the off hand costs the shield; the Holy Symbol is Faith's staff, within the proposal)
- A belt torch sees a bit further than no torch: `beltLight` 0.6 puts its radius, 1.8, above the dark radius, 1.5 (2026-10-17, A belt torch sees a bit further than none)
- One container rule: no container inside another unless empty; big furniture is carried with both hands empty (2026-10-10, late, Containers and carrying)
- Everything is a slot, nothing a pointer; carrying capacity is always a worn item; the hands are slots too (2026-10-10, later, The character; 2026-10-10, after play, The character, Michael's notes)
- Right-click is the complete interface, E the nearest simple action; out-of-range actions auto-walk; no left-click to walk (2026-10-10, Openings)
- A log, 2 by 3, splits into three 1 by 3 planks; Escape closes the top thing, then pauses (2026-10-10, Openings)
- Tab opens the bag and C the character screen, each alone; the stone on those and the belt only (2026-10-12, The bag and the character screen are two panels; 2026-10-12, The stone on three things only)
- The theme is kept: headers on a plate with an X, panels move and remember, the interface capped at 2x (2026-10-12, The theme is kept; 2026-10-12, Finishing touches on the kit)
- The HUD: bars top-left with a portrait, the time top centre, moodles down the right, the log bottom-left (2026-10-12, The HUD rearranged)
- Build is in every right-click menu; Use is first on every item's menu, OSRS style; the text log (2026-10-12, Build in every right-click menu)
- The cursor is CSS, never drawn in the canvas; four choices, the system's the default (2026-10-12, The cursor)
- A real loading screen: not skippable, its stages shown, everything loaded before the title (2026-10-12, The loading shell built)
- Only the pause menu stops the game; a panel open never blocks a fight (2026-10-15, Only the pause menu stops the game)
- Equip and Unequip for anything put on or taken off; the mechanic is Durability; the chat log (2026-10-15, Equip, Unequip, and Durability)
- The map on M: what anyone has seen, kept by the world; every player live; the world runs behind it (2026-10-13, The map's choices, and the long arc)
- Out of a world, the game's website: art infused, usable first, full width, never a box over a world (2026-10-16, The menus and the site)
- Its look: a wordmark of game-like art, a textured menu with PLAY and WIKI, boxes styled from the game (2026-10-16, The menus and the site)
- Behind the site, blurred real screenshots of the game, not a generated placeholder; Michael's painting replaces them (2026-10-16, The menus and the site)
- A loading screen for entering a world, leaving it, and a guest's join (2026-10-16, The menus and the site)
- Worlds, Identities, creation, and Settings are the site's pages; the game fills the window; Exit game returns (2026-10-16, The menus and the site)
- Settings in tabs from a registry; each setting a switch or a dropdown; a Testing tab shown (2026-10-16, The menus and the site)
- The world's rules belong to each world: a Sandbox step of New world, changed by the host from pause (2026-10-16, The menus and the site)
- Two websites for now, the game's and the wiki; later a promotional site, the wiki, and a game client (2026-10-16, The menus and the site)

### Multiplayer

- Play alone (in the browser, offline) or play with friends (on our server, by code), chosen once; no Open to friends (2026-10-17, Play alone or play with friends; telemetry from every world)
- At most 4 players in a browser-hosted world and 8 in a server world, both in data; the browser's goes with browser hosting in #143 (2026-10-16, The needs-michael queue)
- Every addition and every change is built for multiplayer in the same commit (2026-10-13, Multiplayer is live)
- On the wire the world never pauses; a screen is this client's own; hit-stop is refused on the wire (2026-10-13, On the wire the world never pauses)
- The relay server is the transport; the Pages site is the client friends open (2026-10-13, Ruled: the relay server is the transport)
- An identity is a name and a look, reusable; a character belongs to its world, full stop (2026-10-13, Ruled: worlds, identities, characters)
- No world runs until one is chosen; an identity is a stamp; creation is multi-step (2026-10-13, Worlds, identities, characters: the four questions answered)
- An offline guest's character goes dormant after sixty seconds; the store is IndexedDB; longer codes (2026-10-13, The coder agent's review of docs/31/09)
- A guest's panel is a view on its replica; every change is a command by uid, slot, and address (2026-10-13, The twelfth move: a guest's panel as commands)
- Sleep with friends: time skips only when everyone living is in bed (2026-10-13, Sleep in a world with friends)
- A solo world stops while hidden; a world with friends never stops while its host's tab is open (2026-10-13, When the world stops, and when a player steps away; for players a world with friends is a server world from #143, the browser host the test path)
- Five minutes idle in a shared world steps a player away; never in a solo world; never a sleeper (2026-10-13, When the world stops, and when a player steps away; 2026-10-13, A sleeping player never steps away)
- Server worlds for availability: on when anyone is in, everyone a guest, on Fly; single player stays local (2026-10-14, Item 5 ruled)
- The owner's character follows the owner key; a removed player's browser stays barred (2026-10-14, Item 5's two defaults agreed)
- A change on a chunk marks it for the wire or calls `map.unsaved` for the save (2026-10-15, A save that costs what changed)
- The multiplayer core is Done; spectating, lighter frames, and the bounded rewind wait in `05` (2026-10-15, Multiplayer's last core work)
- A guest's own shot is drawn on the bodies' clock, about 67 ms later than before (2026-10-25, The evening's defaults ruled)

### Process and the studio

- The belt is pinned for a discussion of its own before the sittings (held the same evening: 2026-10-25, The belt's slots have roles); potions get a sitting of their own (2026-10-25, The evening's rulings)
- A `net.PROTOCOL` bump in a build Primary runs merges on a clean fresh review, said in the PR's Risks; the rest of `github.md` section 8's list still waits for Michael (2026-10-25, Protocol bumps on review)
- Every tuning lever Michael means to keep turning and every world setting he names has its line in `docs/42-levers.md`, added by the ruling that names it (2026-10-24, The first real melee test)
- The first priority is a final scope for The Valley, with every dev value real and placeholder sounds in (art may lag); the demo is cut from the finished valley (2026-10-18, The Valley's final scope is the first priority; finished means sounds)
- References to real things outside the lore are data, `data/references.json`, shown on one wiki page; the first is the Onion-head Hat, after Cake's Comfort Eagle (2026-10-18, The Onion-head Hat; real-world references on one page; specific names; a lore issue)
- The hidden issues the valley needs are filed again with new scopes; the originals are read and closed when the bot is back (2026-10-18, The hidden issues the valley needs are filed again)
- Recipes are learned and earned (from villagers and the regions; skills by default), not all known from the start (2026-10-18, The valley first; food heals a little; teachers are safe; the wizard; the dead strike what they break)
- The valley and skillscape are the focus; ~~combat testing pauses~~ (reopened 2026-10-23, The melee feel pass: Michael played, and ruled the pass) (2026-10-18, The valley first; food heals a little; teachers are safe; the wizard; the dead strike what they break)
- The roadmap: valley, combat, demo, playing together and telemetry, an outside playtest, Midnight Mass in dev art, the name, art and sound, the Steam page, the regions and release (2026-10-17, The roadmap, and sound)
- Sound off costs something; a visual twin where it makes sense; every sound playable on its wiki page, its license allowing (2026-10-17, The roadmap, and sound)
- Every world is an expendable test until Michael says otherwise; a change may break old saves with no migration (2026-10-17, Every world is an expendable test)
- needs-playtest marks work that waits on Michael's play, not on a decision (2026-10-16, The needs-michael queue)
- 00-vision's "What it is not" gains no new laws; a limit the game needs is a ruling here with its reason (2026-10-16, The four lines of 00-vision.md, ruled)
- A new project, not a rewrite of either parent; new game, new gospel (2026-10-08, The project exists)
- The engine upgrade before more features; small verified moves; nothing in `data/` reformatted (2026-10-12, The engine upgrade is the next task)
- A split of a drawing module is proved by the sheets and the frame hashing the same (2026-10-12, Step 2 of `23` done)
- The dev server keeps connections alive; the atlas is packed tallest first; boot never waits on a frame alone (2026-10-12, The loading shell built)
- Delivered art is copied in as data and read, never run (2026-10-12, The first item art arrives)
- Home is Stardew-shaped, the frontier Zomboid-shaped; the arc is geography, not story (2026-10-13, What this game is)
- Never harder on a clock: no decay, no horde night, no population that grows with the days (2026-10-13, What this game is)
- Skills: ~~one per station family at most~~ (superseded 2026-10-19, The valley has no cure; the wizard lives; the witch teaches Potions; gates stop a harvest; every loot table provisional; two old limits struck: fourteen skills, #275); death takes them, the one real cost of dying; recipes learned and earned; whether a skill may touch combat is open, #275 (2026-10-13, What this game is; 2026-10-18, The valley first; food heals a little; teachers are safe; the wizard; the dead strike what they break; 2026-10-19, Skills may touch combat, open for discussion; death takes them, the one real cost of dying)
- Talk never gates and never writes the world, save a teacher's teaching; bosses gate regions and can be fought again (2026-10-13, What this game is; 2026-10-18, The valley first; food heals a little; teachers are safe; the wizard; the dead strike what they break)
- The long arc: normal until it isn't; no story by design, no end boss, no win screen (2026-10-13, The map's choices, and the long arc)
- The guide is one villager, immune to the dead, who explains the rules (2026-10-13, The guide: one villager)
- Scripted sequences are a beat list under the clock law, never a cutscene; villagers' memory way later (2026-10-13, The map and talk scoped)
- Scripting villagers means more than lines: villagers who move about and bark in response to things, on a set routine, and possibly at a player's command; in-world cutscenes that take the camera and the character are wanted too, against 2026-10-13's "never a cutscene", open (2026-10-18, Day-one food is a tracked statistic; what scripting means)
- Bugs found by the wiki census go to `docs/bugs.md`, confirmed on `main` before fixing (2026-10-14, Bugs found by the wiki census)
- The wiki says what the game is on `main`, every number a reference to the data (2026-10-14, A wiki, scoped)
- The wiki is part of shipping, synced in the same session as the push (2026-10-15, The wiki's rule; 2026-10-15, The wiki is part of shipping)
- Primary and Secondary; a new account for the agents, never `writeplaintext`; `github.md` is the path to `main` (2026-10-15, Primary and Secondary, a bot account; 2026-10-15, `github.md` proposed)
- While the bot is flagged: every session as `michaeldippold`, reviews as signed comments, nothing in bursts (2026-10-15, The process while the bot is flagged)
- Primary's pull requests are reviewed by agents it starts, a standing permission (2026-10-15, Primary's pull requests are reviewed by agents it starts)
- The studio is the organisation `cmzgame`; the infrastructure day is Michael's and Primary's, never through the public repository (2026-10-15, The studio's organisation; 2026-10-15, No friendly fire; the fireball's burst only ahead)
- A doc is a rule, a reference, or a record; records go to `docs/history/`; open work is an issue (2026-10-16, The sweep)
- `05`'s ideas go to GitHub as one tracking issue; the three open design questions become `needs-michael` issues (2026-10-16, The sweep)

### Business

- Every world sends telemetry (anonymous, by default); the legal answer waits for the beta (2026-10-17, Play alone or play with friends; telemetry from every world)
- The free web demo lives forever at a link; the same code in an Electron wrapper on Steam; no rebuild (2026-10-13, The free web demo forever)
- The artist first; Steam is the audience; the demo is a scenario with its own rules, outside the clock law (2026-10-13, The free web demo forever)
- The demo is a proof of concept: supplies for a few days; ~~final art only, not built yet~~ (overturned 2026-10-23, The demo is built on dev art now) built now, on dev art, the game's bounded playtest, and given real art only before it goes public; Endless later (2026-10-13, The demo is a proof of concept; 2026-10-23, The demo is built on dev art now; only its release waits for final art)
- The demo sells the game: the first run reaches the first night; preparation buys time, never escape; a Wishlist button (2026-10-14, The demo sells the game)
- The dead walk in from off screen; the goal is to kill the player; no co-op; "cosy by day" withdrawn (2026-10-14, The demo re-ruled)
- The game needs its name before anything public; then an infrastructure day with the repository private (2026-10-14, The demo re-ruled)
- The private half of the infrastructure day stays a checklist off the repository until the repositories are private (2026-10-16, The sweep)

## Decisions

### 2026-10-26 — Upper floors: a spec; stairs built and placed, not destroyed for now (Michael)

From the research in #511 (what multilevel would take, Zomboid style; Michael's bar: "it's not worth doing if you can't look out a second story window and see zombies on the ground", and bows "as an attack from up high"), Michael asked for a real spec, upper floors first, round rooms (#513) next, roofs (#512) "a distant third", and neither of the first two to wait on roof answers. Then: "Also consider things like third floors, root cellar basements, etc. But if a second floor is the only thing I get out of it, I would still be happy. Also, stairs buildable by players and also exist prefab on map, players cannot destroy them for now so we don't have to make plans for inaccessible second stories if you destroyed the stairs too fast. Destroyable stairs are a nice to have, not an immediate need." The spec is `docs/49`; its defaults are the spec's own, his to overrule.

- **Stairs are built by players and placed on the map,** and **cannot be destroyed for now**, so no upper floor is ever cut off. Destroyable stairs are a later nice-to-have.
- **Third floors and root cellars are designed in;** a second floor alone is enough.
- **The order:** upper floors, then round rooms, then roofs; none waits on roofs.

Later the same night, after `51` and `52` were ruled, Michael asked for "a careful full spec for multiple stories, including a -1 level root cellar under existing houses. Spec out stairs, much like zomboid, 3ish tiles, goes up one floor, magically transitions you to the next layer of the world when you cross the boundary." The spec was rewritten to that: **a stair is three tiles, each rising one crate, and the layer changes as the body crosses the stair's top edge; root cellars sit under existing houses.**

### 2026-10-26 — The demo pass: the dev chest for trying everything, a roomier cottage, mana twice as fast and between health and stamina, real art for the first fifteen minutes (Michael)

Michael, before showing the game to a friend who makes games: "basically a 'make testing easier and more fun' pass."

- **The dev chest:** "Remove all decorations from the dev chest by default except for the bearskin rug and one painting, preferably one of the ones that is two wall segments wide", and "Add to the dev chest most of the things you need to try stuff ... Keep the spread of weapons, but add the skilling items too." It holds every weapon (the throwing knives 40 and axes 20, from 100 each, to make room), every tool (the hatchet, pickaxe, hoe, scythe, shovel, fishing rod, dirk, smith's hammer, shears, lead, and the bucket), the materials most recipes start from, the three potions, six seeds, the bearskin rug, and the wide castle painting; `tools/check.js` proves the list fits its 10 by 14 grid. It stands beside the spawn, two tiles east.
- **Your cottage is roomier:** "Is it possible to give it more tiles inside so it's not so cramped?" 11 by 7 inside, from 7 by 5: the living room 7 wide, the bedroom 4, two more chairs, and the yard and fence grown with it. *Primary's reading:* the spawn moved to the living room by the door, so the guide outside stays within three tiles of it (`docs/34`).
- **Mana regenerates twice as fast:** "Double the mana regen rate. This is probably permanent." `player.manaRegen` 0.667 a second, two points every three seconds (`docs/42`).
- **The mana bar sits between health and stamina:** "move mana up and stamina to the bottom of that stack."
- **Real art for the first fifteen minutes:** "Anything that you'd see in the first 15 minutes of play should ideally have real art, excluding dev box and bag. Barrel is one of the oldest models in the game." Drawn this time by Primary's own art agents (art round 4) rather than Claude Design, from a brief written to its house style (`tools/art-gen/round4/`, the previews in `docs/art/previews/round4/`): the weapons, the tools, the food and materials a player meets first, and the home's barrel, chest, shelf, and woodpile. Each item came as its icon, its picture, and for a held thing its portrait; what was left generated, and why, is in the pull request.

### 2026-10-26 — Waking early, and Burn corpse by a lit torch (Michael)

Asked on #508's review whether a sleeper with a friend awake (the world at 1, so a night is about four real minutes of a black screen with no way out; a sleeper otherwise gets up only rested and in the morning) should be able to wake, and whether "or Use a lit torch on a corpse" covers the menu's Burn corpse too, Michael: "Yes: any key or click wakes you early, with the fade back in; and the menu's Burn corpse works with a lit torch in hand too."

- **Waking early:** while asleep, any key or click on your own client wakes you (a key event in your snapshot, `wake`, performed by the world's step on the host, so a guest wakes the same way); the screen fades back in, and the world's speed follows who is still asleep, as it always has. The press that put you to bed never wakes you (*the builder's reading*: a press counts only once your client has seen you asleep a step).
- **Burn corpse from the menu** is offered and works with a lit torch in a hand as well as a tinderbox carried, greyed only with neither ("Burn corpse (need a tinderbox or a lit torch)"); nothing is spent either way. `docs/43` section 7.

### 2026-10-26 — No browser dialog: every question is the game's own, on the page (Michael)

Michael, on the Worlds screen's Delete that did nothing: "I almost certainly misclicked and had the browser stop showing me those alerts and that is not easy to undo. Can we stop relying on browser alerts and use our own that can't be refused, since they are in game context and you can stop playing at any time or just not do the thing if you don't want the alert." #507 had already replaced the seven `window.confirm` questions with questions asked on the page; this makes it a rule: the game never calls `window.alert`, `confirm`, or `prompt`, and `tools/check.js` fails on one (`src/CLAUDE.md`, Rules the code keeps).

### 2026-10-26 — Fill waterskins skips clean water (Michael)

Asked on #507 whether Fill waterskins should go on topping up the clean starting skin with tainted water (the auto-sip drinks only clean water, so the clean skin is the one never full, and the next Fill at a well tainted it all), Michael: "Yes: Fill waterskins at a tainted source skips any skin holding clean water; an empty or tainted one still fills." When every skin that could take water holds clean water, the line says why: "Your waterskins hold clean water: they won't be filled from tainted water." (*Primary's wording.*) A bucket's fill is unchanged. `docs/44` section 1.

### 2026-10-26 — Throwing knives do 18 (Michael)

From his playtest, of the knife's damage: "Make throwing knives do 18 damage, your initial suggestion, 15 is too low." The knife's `thrown.damage` in `data/items.json` is 18 (nine throws to kill one of the dead at 150, where 15 took ten); everything else of 2026-10-25's ruling stands. `docs/24` section 1 and `docs/42` carry the number.

### 2026-10-26 — The playtest's rulings: a bucket boiled and poured, burning the dead for a tinderbox alone, sleep fades and lasts longer, bags open where they lie, stacks fill a stack (Michael)

From his playtest reports on the Playtest Checklist. Primary answered each with a recommendation; Michael adopted them with two changes of his own.

- **A bucket is boiled and poured** ("Adopted"): a bucket of tainted water is boiled at any lit cooking fire, the fireplace included, as a skin is, its bar longer (*default:* 60 s); Use a bucket on a waterskin fills the skin from it. Clean into clean, or tainted into tainted or into an empty skin, is fine; tainted into clean is refused, "That would taint the clean water." Michael: "Mostly what I want here is to make it so you can boil a bucket of water and distribute it to waterskins, should you prefer that over boiling each waterskin separately."
- **Burning the dead costs nothing but a flame:** "Let's just make it have no cost but a tinderbox, or 'Use' a lit torch on a corpse should also do this." Burn corpse needs a tinderbox carried and spends nothing; Use a lit torch on a body of the dead burns it too. (`docs/43` section 7's log is struck.)
- **Sleep fades to black** ("Adopted"): the screen fades to black over about a second as you sleep and back in as you wake; you stand beside the bed, about to get in or just got out; with friends only your own screen fades, and the others see you beside the bed with a "Zz" over you. A lying-down animation stays a someday. **And a night's sleep lasts longer:** "Let's also consider slowing down the clock a little for sleep, right now it only takes a couple of seconds for a full sleep." Today `player.sleepTimeScale` 60 runs eight game hours in four real seconds (a day is 720 s); *default:* 20, about twelve seconds for a night, a lever.
- **A container item opens where it lies** ("Adopted"): any item that holds things (a belt, the satchel, the rucksack) has Open on the ground and in a chest, with Take All, as a body has; no need to equip it to move what is in it.
- **A stack dropped on a matching stack fills it** ("Adopted"): in a hand, a belt slot, or a bag cell, up to the stack limit, the rest staying where it came from; entries that carry their own state (wear, colours, a fill, a coat) never merge. A split control can come later.

### 2026-10-26 — Honey goes into a clay jar: made like a vial, the jar back when it is eaten, a couple at the cottage (Michael)

From his playtest, on the checklist: "Sidling up to a beehive, taking honey, and getting an item called 'pot of honey' is really confusing now that we have you gathering vials for potions. It's a free pot vending machine? ... I think you should have to have pots, give maybe one for free at your house, rest are made?" Primary recommended a clay jar made as a vial is; Michael: "Honey goes into an empty clay jar, made like a vial; eating leaves the jar; a couple of jars at the cottage to start."

- **Taking honey needs an empty clay jar** carried; it fills the jar. Without one it is refused: "You need a clay jar to take honey." (Michael: "It should also refuse to take honey if no container.")
- **A clay jar is made like a vial:** clay shaped at the workbench and fired in an oven (the clay ruling of 2026-10-23; `docs/47` section 2).
- **Eating the honey leaves the empty jar**, as drinking a potion leaves its vial; so does honey spent in a recipe (pemmican's binder, the stamina potion's secondary).
- **Your cottage starts with a couple of empty jars.**
- *Primary's defaults:* one clay shapes one unfired jar (a vial is two to a clay; a jar is bigger), fired to a clay jar, 1 by 1, stacking 10 as the vial does; the item becomes the **Jar of honey**; two empty jars in the cottage's chest; the hives stay open to all (only a hive of your own is Beastlore 5, already ruled).

### 2026-10-25 — Sight-pull ruled: one of the dead drawn in calls once to those that see it, with no chain; sound stays out (Michael)

Asked whether a pulled zombie alerts its neighbours, and whether sound draws the dead (it does not: they notice a player they see, a flame at night, and a blow), Michael ruled: "I think we need sight-pull to replace Zomboid's sound-pull. And I think this is especially crucial for long range weapons that go further than any other way a zombie could find you, so it's a safe attack on one zombie at a time. If you have social aggro, it stops being safe and adds some of the pressure back that not caring about sound is getting us. I think sound never being an aggro thing is probably the right call but who knows in the future." Shown the two ways to build it (a pulled one broadcasting once, or the idle dead watching for a chase), he chose: "Let's do the one time broadcast to nearby then no further chain reaction as you suggested." The spec is `docs/48`.

- **The pull:** when one of the dead first turns to hunt a player, first-hand (it sees one, or a player's blow lands and it lives), it calls once to the dead within a few tiles that can see it, scaled by the light as a player's sight is; they join the hunt toward where it learnt of the player. Hunting a player is its own mark (`huntsPlayer`, Primary's reading), so one first drawn by a flame or a villager still pulls when it sees the player.
- **No chain:** the ones it calls never call others; the call is made once, at the turn, never again while that hunt lasts.
- **Sound is never aggro**, for now; the Noise someday stays open (`docs/05`).

### 2026-10-25 — Throwing knives and axes ruled: the knife pulls, the axe stops, they spin, and they break like the spear (Michael)

Asked whether the throwing knives and axes (drawn, never thrown) had an issue, Michael wanted them to "work pretty much like the spear, low damage but perhaps an increased chance to knock down", the axe doing more damage and costing more to make, and asked whether a knockdown spoils them for pulling the dead at range. Primary answered that it does not (a floored one still hunts the thrower and comes when it is up; a knockdown only holds it off longer) and proposed a job for each. Michael: "Drop axe damage to 30 and knife to 15. Otherwise adopted, you can write the spec. Also make sure we get a spinning knife or axe art to send along the arc because it'll look awesome." And on where they end up: "on bodies of dead zombies or ground like arrows? I think yes, with knives having a 30% chance to break, and axes like a 10% chance to break". The spec is `docs/24` section 1.

- **The knife pulls:** ~~15 damage~~ (18 since 2026-10-26, Throwing knives do 18), 13 tiles, fast and flat, a 0.3 stagger chance, no knockdown, 30% to break, stacks of 20.
- **The axe stops:** 30 damage, 9 tiles, a short arc, always staggers, knocks down at 0.35, 10% to break, stacks of 10.
- **They spin along the arc**, lodge in a body or land on the ground like arrows, and are swept up with E.
- **Made at Blacksmithing 3**, the axe dearer: *Primary's default*, one iron bar makes ten knives, an iron bar and a plank two axes, so a knife's throw costs less than an axe's at these breaks (`24` has the arithmetic).

### 2026-10-25 — Potions, poisons, and mana ruled: clay vials, clean water, two herbs and a secondary at a cauldron, mana 100 and the Fire Bolt 20, poisons put on with Use, a splash for every potion (Michael)

The potions sitting (Michael placed it first among the sittings, 2026-10-25), with poisons and a mana bar wrapped in. His questions were the supply ("some real busywork ... else people are going to be drowning in potions"), whether glass and sand were needed, what spells cost ("Fire bolt no longer two shots zombies ... So maybe 20 mana?"), a station or on the go, herbs and secondaries, poison levels and stacking, the splash, and blocking a drink of poison. Primary answered each with a recommendation; Michael: "I actually do not have a single change to suggest now. Anything that might need to change from the spec can be discovered in playtesting." He added that a poison is put on through Use: "I forgot about arrows and blades, that is a perfect case for our 'use' system." The spec is `docs/47`; its defaults are Primary's numbers and readings.

- **Vials are clay**, under the clay ruling of 2026-10-23: shaped at the workbench, fired in any oven; no glass, no sand. Drinking leaves the empty vial; throwing shatters it.
- **A potion needs clean water**, boiled (`44`), and is brewed **at a cauldron over a lit fire, never on the go**: the witch's at the mere first, your own made at the anvil later.
- **Two of a potion's main herb and one secondary**; each potion its own main herb, the secondaries shared: healing yarrow and sage, stamina nettle and honey, mana mageroot and a mushroom, poison nightshade and grave moss, strong poison wolfsbane and grave moss.
- **Mana:** a bar of 100, raised later by gear; slow regeneration; the mana potion gives 50; the altar's prayer refills it; a cast the bar cannot pay is refused with a log line. **Spells cost by their power:** the Fire Bolt 20 (the book of fire's spell, "Fireball" in the data); a utility 5 to 10, a bolt about 20, the big ones 35 or more.
- **Poisons are never drunk:** Use a poison on a blade to coat it for about ten hits, or on arrows for about five poisoned arrows. Two tiers, ordinary and strong; a poisoned hit adds a stack, up to three, refreshing the timer; poison never spreads and lights nothing; **it works on the dead.**
- **Every potion splashes when thrown** (Potions 7): healing and mana help everyone in the area, poison stacks on the dead and wild game in it; a harmful splash never hurts a player.

### 2026-10-25 — Right- or left-handed: chosen once, a label and a drawn arm, never mechanical (Michael)

Michael asked: "since main and off hand are not quite synonymous with right hand and left hand respectively, can we offer players a setting in the start screen for 'right handed or left handed', never ambidextrous because that would kill the main and off distinction. Default to right handed." Then, narrowing it: "this should be a choose it once at character screen, mostly for flavor, and all it changes is the hand icon stamped in the slots (by default it's a right hand in the main slots) and which of your right and left hands are called main hand and off hand in your character screen. It's basically just a label for main and off, everything else cares about that distinction, never left or right." And clarifying: "Labels, icons, plus drawn matching arm! … Mostly I wanted to stress that all of these are visual and mechanical is still all the main/off thing."

So a character is right-handed or left-handed (`handed`, "right" by default; no third choice). What it changes is visual: the character screen calls the hands by side, the belt's hand icons show the matching hand (a right hand in a main-hand slot for the right-handed, a left hand for the left-handed, the other hand in an off-hand slot), and the figure draws the main hand's thing in the matching arm with every pose on that side. The sim, combat, the belt's choices, and the mouse buttons (the left button is always the main hand, `docs/21`) read main and off and never left or right.

*Primary's reading:* handedness belongs to the character, so every player sees which arm holds the sword (it rides in the player's record and the save); it is chosen on the identity screen beside the looks and stamped on each character made from the identity, as the look is.
